Algorithm "convolution flattening":
- Initialize a (maybe base p).
- For multiple iterations:
  - Compute conv coefficients c=ifft(fft(a)^2)[:2n-1] (linear).
  - Define target c_t: lower peaks, maybe c_t_k = min(c_k, λ * median?) or with soft clipping.
  - Compute frequency ratio R(ω)=sqrt(c_t(ω)/c(ω))? Here c(ω)=FFT(c) = B^2. Actually c(ω)=B(ω)^2. The ratio of target/actual in frequency is C_t(ω)/C(ω). We can update B_new = B * phase? Since B^2 = C (as polynomial zero-padded), C(ω)=B(ω)^2. If target C_t not exactly square, choose square root preserving current phase: B_new(ω)=B(ω)*sqrt(C_t(ω)/C(ω)) (complex square root). Then a_new=ifft(B_new)[:n], clip nonnegative. This is a projection from time-domain target to a.
- Normalize.
This directly attacks c coefficients. It might converge to a sequence with flatter convolution. Similar to previous state-of-art spectral flattening? It flattens C_t in time, not |B|^2. But C(ω)=B^2, so previous flattened |B|^2 = time c? No, |B|^2 is power spectrum of a, whose inverse is circular autocorrelation, not convolution. For real a not symmetric, |B|^2 = B(ω)B*(ω), while C(ω)=B(ω)^2. Different! Our method uses B^2, appropriate.

- Main optimization engine:
   1. Time-domain conv flattening (projective GF): For each starting sequence, iterate:
       - a normalized sum1.
       - For iter in range(time): compute c=conv(a) (sum1). Determine target c_t. Options:
           * Hard clamp at threshold T: c_t = min(c, T * scale) but sum not 1; normalize sum to1? If we just clamp and renormalize, target distribution with max T/(sum after normalization)?            * Use a soft "plateau": choose current max M, target = min(c, (1-alpha)*M + beta*mean?)? Since sum normalize.
       - Update B = rfft(a,M); C = B*B. C_t = C - ifft? Actually target in frequency: Ct_fft = rfft(c_t, M). Then ratio = Ct_fft / C; B_new = B * sqrt(ratio). Need handle zeros: `ratio = Ct_fft / (C + eps * abs(C))`. Since C complex. sqrt complex principal. Then a_new = irfft(B_new,M)[:n]. Clip to 0, normalize. Score update. This is basically projecting desired distribution.
       - We can perform multiple subiterations with decreasing alpha. The target choice critical. If target is too flat (uniform), square root may produce negative; clipping helps. Use phases preserving current.
   This method can be run many iterations fast.

   Is it guaranteed to reduce max? Not necessarily; but with proper target and maybe line search on blend. Let's revisit continuous target and maybe derive a better initial via polynomial square root of a target with adjustable shape. We can compute causal square root of an arbitrary target polynomial C(z) of degree up to 2n-2 using a fast algorithm. This can directly produce a sequence with convolution near C. If we choose C with coefficients all equal to 1 (uniform), causal sqrt truncated gives base p. But we can choose C coefficients not uniform but with shape that makes its causal square root have lower max. What shape of C is the convolution of some nonnegative A? We can iteratively optimize C: fix A, C=A^2; modify C to lower max; compute A'=sqrt(C'); iterate. This is the time-domain flattening method. It is essentially alternating between C and A. It may converge to a fixed point where C is as flat as possible within the set of squares.

How to compute causal square root of C efficiently and robustly? Standard: if C[0]>0, A = sqrt(C) polynomial using recurrence: 2a_k = c_k - ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}. O(n^2) too slow. But using FFT log: A(z)=exp(0.5 log C(z)). For polynomial C that is near a square, we can compute with current A phase method: B_new = B * sqrt(C'_fft / C_fft). This only works if C' close to C; iterative small steps. For large target (uniform from p maybe okay). We can also compute from scratch via Newton: Given C, compute A with length n satisfying A^2=C? def time_flatten(a0, max_seconds, seed=0, mode_patterns=None):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0.copy()); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L=2*n-1; Mfft=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    # use top-level fast_conv_score returns c, sc?
    it=0
    start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+max_seconds
    no_improve=0
    # parameters: choose transform modes cycling: ['clamp','pow','uniform','quantile']
    while time.monotonic()<limit:
       c=fast_conv(a); sc=2*n*c.max()/S_s^2 (score from c maybe S=1)
       if sc<best_sc: ... no_improve=0 else no_improve++
       if c.max() <= lower? break
       # generate 2-4 targets
       for mode in cycles:
          c_t = make_target(c, mode, it, rng)
          cand = spectral_newton(a, B? c_t, Mfft) # compute B maybe once
          # compute B once outside
          score_cand = score(cand)
          # If score_cand < best_sc or < sc? accept with blend
          if score_cand < sc or score_cand < best_sc: accept
          else try blend 0.5
       if no_improve > 20: perturb/restart? a = normalize(a + small noise); no_improve=0

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def next_power_two(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()

def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    M=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:L]
    return c
```
For n maybe 1, L=1, M=1? bit_length of 0? `(L-1).bit_length()` for L=1: 0.bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. ok.

```
def compute_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(S*S))
```
If a normalized, S=1.

```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-300: return a
    return a/S
```

Base p:
```
def make_p_n(n, rev=False, offset=1.0):
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)+offset
    a=x**-0.5
    if rev: a=a[::-1]
    return normalize(a)
```
Use recurrence? x**-0.5 fine. For huge maybe no.

       a=(1e-6 + x)**exp * ? Maybe shift=0.05

   seqs=[]
   if parent is not None:
      p=interpolate_to_n(parent,n)
      seqs.append(normalize(p))
   seqs.append(make_p_n(n))
   seqs.append(make_p_n(n, rev=True))
   seqs.append(normalize((make_p_n(n)+make_p_n(n,rev=True))/2))
   for e in [-0.5,-0.35,-0.2,0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0]:
      seqs.append(make_power_seed(n,e))
      seqs.append(make_power_seed(n,e,rev=True))
   for alpha in [0.5,1,2,5]:

      seqs.append(normalize(np.exp(-alpha*x)))
      seqs.append(normalize(np.exp(-alpha*(1-x))))
   # maybe Gaussian
   for center in [0.2,0.5,0.8]: ...

Time-flatten function:
```
def make_target(c, mode, it, rng):
    L=c.size; c=c.copy()
    # normalize to sum1
    s=c.sum(); if s<=0: return c/L; c=c/s
    if mode==0:
       # clip to quantile q varies 0.8,0.9,0.97
       q=0.85 + 0.1*np.sin(0.01*it) maybe
       T=np.quantile(c, q)
       c=np.minimum(c,T)
       c += (1.0 - c.sum())/L
    elif mode==1:
       alpha=0.7 + 0.2*((it%50)/50)
       c=np.power(np.maximum(c,0), alpha)
       c/=c.sum()
    elif mode==2:
       lam=0.1 + 0.3*((it%100)/100)
       c=(1-lam)*c + lam/L
       c/=c.sum()
    elif mode==3:
       q=0.95
       T=np.quantile(c,q)
       below_q=np.quantile(c,0.25)
       mask=c<T # lows
       c2=np.minimum(c,T)
       deficit=1-c2.sum()
       lows=c2<bels
       if lows.sum()>0: c2[lows]+=deficit/lows.sum()
       else c2+=deficit/L
       c=c2
    else: c = c/L
    c=np.maximum(c,0); c/=c.sum()
    return c
```
Need deterministic but can randomize q.

`flatten_update(a,B,c_t,Mfft,damp)`:
```
def spectral_candidate(a, B, c_t, Mfft, damp):
    Ct=np.fft.rfft(c_t, Mfft)
    Bsafe=B.copy()
    small=np.abs(Bsafe)<1e-20
    Bsafe[small]=1e-20
    Bnew=0.5*(Bsafe + Ct/Bsafe)
    Bupd=B + damp*(Bnew-B)
    # ensure real-ish? rfft fine
    cand=np.fft.irfft(Bupd, Mfft)[:len(a)]
    cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
    S=cand.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return None
    cand/=S
    return cand
```
Maybe if B has negative real etc.

def lse_score_grad_a(a, beta):
    n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a); S=a.sum(); if S<...; c_shifted=beta*(c-c.max()); exp=np.exp(np.clip(c_shifted,-50,50)); p=exp/exp.sum(); L=c.max()+np.log(exp.sum())/beta
    # grad_L
    a_rev=a[::-1]
    M3= (2*n-1)+n-1
    M3fft=1 << (M3-1).bit_length()
    conv_res=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p,M3fft)*np.fft.rfft(a_rev,M3fft), M3fft)[:M3]
    grad_L=2.0*conv_res[n-1:2*n-1]
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad=2.0*n*(grad_L/S2 - 2.0*L/S3)  # vector ones? need multiply ones: `grad_L/S2 - 2.0*L/S3 * np.ones`
    score=2.0*n*L/S2
    return score, grad
```
Need `grad = (2*n)*(grad_L/S2 - 2.0*L/S3)`? In Python scalar subtract works? Must add array: `term = grad_L / S2 - (2.0 * L / S3) * np.ones(n,)`; grad=2n*term. If S=1, simple.

`lse_b_obj(b, beta)`:

def lse_b_objective(b, beta):
    b=b - b.mean()
    b_max=b.max(); a=np.exp(b-b_max); S=a.sum(); if S<...: return 1e30, zeros
    a/=S
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_a(a,beta)
    h=ga - np.dot(a,ga)
    g=a*h
    return sc,g
```
Need return tuple for minimize with jac=True. Top-level function cannot have *args? Use `def lse_b_objective(x, beta):` and `minimize(..., args=(beta,), jac=True)`. It returns (f,g). Good.

Alternative use exact subgradient/coordinate descent final:
```
def coordinate_peak_cut(a, n_sweeps):
  # For top active peaks, reduce coordinates that contribute. Try line search.
```
Previous code had coord but used peak aut? It assumed peak index and pairs via `a[js] * a[k_star - js]` which for convolution index k with i+j=k; if k < n? For full k 0..2n-2. Need correct. Can implement a generic local search:
- Compute c, select top K peaks (indices). For each coordinate i, try multiply by factor f (0.5,0.7,0.9,1.2,1.5 maybe) and update score. Since changing one coordinate affects c_k for k in [i, i+n-1]. We can compute updated c in O(n) using rank-one update: c' = c + (new-old)*(a_{k-i}) over k range. This is efficient! Coordinate descent with rank-one updates can refine exact max.

This is valuable. Let's implement coordinate descent for exact objective:
Current a length n normalized. c=fast_conv(a). Score=2n max c / S^2. Changing a_j by delta affects sum S. The score is scale invariant; but if we maintain normalization after each change. To evaluate coordinate changes efficiently, we can update c for each j exactly O(n) without FFT. For n=10000 and many coordinates maybe okay if few.

For one coordinate j old v, new w. Let delta=w-v. c_k changes by delta*(a_{k-j} for k-j in [0,n-1]) + delta*(a_{j-(k-n+1?})? Wait linear convolution index k=i+j; changing a_j affects terms with i+j=k and j2+k? Actually c_k=∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. Let's write `make_target` deterministic with mode and progress:
```
def make_target(c, mode, prog, rng):
   c=np.maximum(c,0); c=c/c.sum()
   if mode==0: # quantile clip increasing hardness
      q=0.80 + 0.18*prog
      T=np.quantile(c,q)
      c=np.minimum(c,T)
      c += (1.0-c.sum())/c.size
   elif mode==1: # power
      alpha=0.6 + 0.3*prog
      c=np.power(c+1e-30, alpha)
   elif mode==2: # uniform blend
      lam=0.05 + 0.45*prog
      c=(1-lam)*c + lam/c.size
   elif mode==3: # top-flat low-boost
      q=0.9
      T=np.quantile(c,q)
      c2=np.minimum(c,T)
      deficit=1-c2.sum()
      low_count=max(1,int(0.5*c.size))
      low_idx=np.argpartition(c2, low_count)[:low_count]
      c2[low_idx]+=deficit/low_count
   else: c=np.full_like(c,1.0/c.size)
   c=np.maximum(c,0); c/=c.sum()
   return c
```
`np.argpartition` O(L) okay. For mode3 low_count half.

`flatten_one(a, B, c_t, Mfft, damp)` returns candidate.

`time_flatten` main:
```
def time_flatten(a0, max_seconds, seed=0):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0.copy()); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    L=2*n-1; M=...
    start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+max_seconds
    it=0; no_improve=0
    modes=[0,2,1,3]
    while time.monotonic()<limit:
       c=fast_conv(a)
       maxc=float(c.max()); S=1? a normalized but after normalize sum=1.
       sc=2*n*maxc
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve+=1
       if maxc <= 0: break
       if no_improve > 80:
           # perturb best? Or use current with noise
           a = normalize(a + 0.002*rng.standard_normal(n))
           no_improve=0
           if time ... break
       B=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
       prog=min(1.0, it/500)
       candidate_best=None; candidate_sc=best_sc
       for m in (modes[it%4], modes[(it+1)%4]):
          c_t=make_target(c, m, prog, rng)
          Ct=np.fft.rfft(c_t, M)
          for damp in (1.0, 0.35):
             Bsafe=B.copy(); small=abs(Bsafe)<1e-18; Bsafe[small]=1e-18
             Bnew=0.5*(Bsafe + Ct/Bsafe)
             Bupd=B + damp*(Bnew - B)  # if Bsmall changed? use B as base
             cand=np.fft.irfft(Bupd,M)[:n]
             cand=np.maximum(cand,0); S=cand.sum()
             if S<1e-12: continue
             cand/=S
             sc_c=compute_score(cand)
             if sc_c < candidate_sc:
                candidate_sc=sc_c; candidate_best=cand.copy()
             # Maybe if sc_c<sc accept immediately
       if candidate_best is not None and (candidate_sc<sc or candidate_sc<best_sc or it<20):
           a=normalize(candidate_best); if candidate_sc<best_sc: best_sc=...; no_improve=0
       else:
           # small move toward candidate even if not improving?
           if candidate_best is not None:
              a=normalize(0.8*a + 0.2*candidate_best)
              sc=compute_score(a); update
           # else disturb
       it+=1
    return best _, best_sc
```

Coordinate descent function:
```
def coordinate_descent(a0, max_seconds, top_peaks=20, top_coords=50):
    a=np.asarray(a0,dtype=float); # don't normalize? Keep scale invariant but convenient sum1
    a=normalize(a); S=1.0
    L=2*n-1; c=fast_conv(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=2*n*c.max()
    start=... limit
    rng=...
    no_improve=0
    while time<limit:
       peaks=np.argsort(c)[-top_peaks:]
       # candidate coords: for each peak, indices j where j in [max(0,k-n+1), min(k,n-1)]. Weight by a_j*a_{k-j}.
       weights=np.zeros(n)
       for k in peaks:
          js=range(max(0,k-n+1), min(k,n-1)+1)
          m=np.arange(start,end+1)
          weights[ids] += a[ids]*a[k-ids]
       cand_idx=np.argsort(weights)[-top_coords:]
       improved=False
       for j in cand_idx:
          v=a[j]
          if v<1e-15: try increase small? factors [0.1,0.3,0.5]? Can add mass.
          factors=[0.3,0.6,0.85,1.1,1.5,2.0] maybe if v small.
          best_f=1; best_sc_cur=best_sc; best_vec=None; best_w=v; best_S=S
          for f in factors:
             w=v*f; if w<0 continue
             delta=w-v
             idx0=max(0,j-n+1); idx1=min(j+n-1,2*n-2)
             if idx1<idx0: continue
             m=np.arange(idx0, idx1+1) - j
             vec=2*delta*a[m]
             if 2*j<L:
                pos=2*j
                vec[pos-idx0] = delta*(v+w)  # because for k=2j
             c_new=c+vec
             S_new=S+delta
             if S_new<=1e-12: continue
             sc=2*n*np.max(c_new)/(S_new*S_new)
             # keep best for this coordinate maybe
             if sc<best_sc: improved=True; best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new? Need update a/c after loop.
             if sc<best_sc_cur: best_sc_cur=sc; best_vec=vec; best_w=w; best_S=S_new
          if best_sc_cur < current_sc - 1e-12:
             # apply
             a[j]=best_w; c+=best_vec; S=best_S; current_sc=...
       # after coordinate pass, maybe normalize a to prevent growth; c scales? If normalize, c = c * (oldS/newS)^2? Since score invariant but c values change. Simpler: if S >2 or <0.5, rescale a=S_target/S, c*= (S_target/S)^2, S=S_target.
       if no improvement over pass: perturb or break.
    return normalize(best_a), compute_score(normalize(best_a))
```

If later another coordinate changed, c is updated based on old a (with previous changes) because we update a vector when accepting. For candidate evaluation of multiple factors for same j, if not accepted, a unchanged; if accept after loop, we use best_vec computed with old a. Good.
If we accept and move to next j, c and a updated; weights not recomputed until next pass.
Could be O(pass*coords*factors*L). 50*6*20000=6M ops per pass, plus argsort. okay.

def optimize_lse(a0, max_seconds, seed=0):
    start=time; limit=start+max_seconds
    a=normalize(a0); best_sc=score(a); best_a=a
    betas=[10,100,1000,10000,100000,500000]
    b=np.log(a+1e-15); b=b-b.mean()
    for beta in betas:
       if time>limit-30 break
       remaining=limit-time-30
       maxiter=int(min(4000, 200 + remaining*3))
       try res=minimize(... maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxiter*20)
       a=softmax(res.x); update
       if time... break
    # After LSE, maybe one pass with best beta smaller? 
    return best_a,best_sc
```

`score_from_conv(a,c)`: assume c length, S sum.

```
def score_from_conv(a,c):
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=1e-12: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c))/(S*S))
```
But if a normalized in time_flatten S=1.

if len(a)==n: return a.copy()

return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)

`initial_sequences(n, parent)`:

```
def initial_sequences(n, parent):
    seqs=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p=interpolate_to_n(parent, n)
       p=normalize(p)
       if p.size==n and p.sum()>0: seqs.append(p)
       # noise parents
       rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
       for sc in (0.01,0.03): seqs.append(normalize(p*(1+sc*rng.standard_normal(n))))
    seqs.append(make_p_n(n))
    seqs.append(make_p_n(n, True))
    seqs.append(normalize((make_p_n(n)+make_p_n(n,True))/2.0))
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    xn=(x+0.5)/n
    for e in (-0.6,-0.45,-0.3,-0.15,0.05,0.3,0.6,1.0):
        if e<0:
           base=1e-4 + x/(n+1.0) # or (x+1)
           # Use index power: (x+1)^e to avoid zero
           a=(x+1.0)**e
        else:
           a=xn**e
        seqs.append(normalize(a))
        seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))
    for alpha in (0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0):
       a=np.exp(-alpha*xn)
       seqs.append(normalize(a)); seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))
    # Mixtures: p + reverse? Gaussian
    for center,sigma in [(0.1,0.1),(0.3,0.2),(0.5,0.25),(0.7,0.2)]:
       a=np.exp(-((xn-center)**2)/(2*sigma*sigma))
       seqs.append(normalize(a)); seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))
    # Binary random? Maybe low density p?
    rng=np.random.default_rng(2)
    for rho,amp in [(0.1,1.0),(0.2,1.0)]:
       mask=rng.random(n)<rho
       a=np.where(mask,1.0,0.0)
       if a.sum()>0: seqs.append(normalize(a))
    # Deduplicate? not necessary
    return seqs
```
For e<0, using (x+1)**e gives p-like. For e=-0.5 good. For e positive, using xn**e maybe.

def time_flatten(a0, max_seconds, seed=1):
    a=normalize(np.asarray(a0,dtype=np.float64))
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return a, np.inf
    L=2*n-1; M=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+max_seconds
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it=0; no_improve=0
    last_report=0
    modes=[0,2,1,3]
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        c=fast_conv(a)
        S=a.sum()
        maxc=float(np.max(c))
        if S<1e-12: break
        sc=2.0*n*maxc/(S*S)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
            if it - last_report > 50: print...; last_report=it
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 120:
            # kick: mix best with random positive noise
            a = normalize(0.85*best_a + 0.15*normalize(1.0 + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n)))? This could be bad.
            # Or restart from best
            a = normalize(best_a * (1.0 + 0.005*rng.standard_normal(n)))
            no_improve=0
            if no_improve >? break? 
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 0.5: break
        prog = min(1.0, it/400.0)
        B = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        candidate_best=None; candidate_sc=best_sc
        c_norm = c / c.sum()  # should sum1 if S=1? For general S, c sum S^2; normalize for target.
        for mode in (modes[it%4], modes[(it+1)%4]):
            c_t = make_target(c_norm, mode, prog, rng)
            Ct = np.fft.rfft(c_t, M)
            # try damps depending on progress
            damps=(1.0,0.4)
            for damp in damps:
                Bnew = B.copy()
                eps=1e-12*max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(B))))
                mask=np.abs(B) > eps
                Bnew[mask] = 0.5*(B[mask] + Ct[mask]/B[mask])
                Bupd = B + damp*(Bnew - B)
                cand = np.fft.irfft(Bupd, M)[:n]
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                S_c = cand.sum()
                if S_c < 1e-12: continue
                cand = cand / S_c
                sc_c = compute_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < candidate_sc:
                    candidate_sc=sc_c
                    candidate_best=cand.copy()
        if candidate_best is not None and (candidate_sc < sc or candidate_sc < best_sc or it < 30):
            a = normalize(candidate_best)
            if candidate_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=candidate_sc; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            # take a small step toward best candidate if any; else noise
            if candidate_best is not None:
                a = normalize(0.85*a + 0.15*candidate_best)
                sc=compute_score(a)
                if sc < best_sc: update
            else:
                a = normalize(a + 0.001*rng.standard_normal(n))
        it += 1
    return best_a, best_sc
```

`optimize_lse` b objective:
Need top-level `softmax_b(b)`:
```
def softmax_b(b):
    b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    b=np.clip(b, -50.0, 50.0)
    b=b - b.mean()
    b=b - b.max()
    a=np.exp(b)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones_like(b)/b.size
    return a/s
```

`lse_b_fun(b, beta)`:
```
b=np.asarray(b)
a=softmax_b(b)

h=ga - np.dot(a,ga)
g=a*h
if not finite: return 1e30, np.zeros_like(b)
return sc,g
```

def coordinate_descent(a0, max_seconds, seed=0):
    a=normalize(copy)
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    c=fast_conv(a)
    S=1.0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    rng=...
    limit=...
    no_pass_improve=0
    pass_count=0
    top_peaks=20; top_coords=80
    while time<limit:
        # rescale to sum1 and c scaled (should)
        S=float(a.sum())
        if S<=... break
        scale=1.0/S
        if abs(scale-1)>1e-9:
            a=a*scale; c=c*(scale*scale); S=1.0
        c=np.maximum(c,0)? Conv should positive but FFT error; maybe clip small negative.
        sc_cur=2*n*np.max(c)
        if sc_cur < best_sc: update
        peaks=np.argsort(c)[-top_peaks:]
        weights=np.zeros(n)
        for k in peaks:
           k=int(k)
           st=max(0,k-n+1); en=min(k,n-1)
           ids=np.arange(st,en+1)
           if ids.size:
              # contribution a[ids]*a[k-ids]
              weights[ids] += a[ids]*a[k-ids]
        # Add some random candidates to explore
        cand_idx=np.argsort(weights)[-top_coords:]
        if top_coords < n:
           extra=rng.choice(n, min(20,n), replace=False)
           cand_idx=np.unique(np.append(cand_idx,extra))
        improved=False
        current_sc=sc_cur
        for j in cand_idx:

           if v < 1e-15 and rng.random()>0.1: continue
           # factors
           if v < 1e-6:
              factors=(0.1,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0) # can't multiply by 0 if zero? factor*0=0. Need add? If v zero, factor no effect. Use absolute additions.
           else:

                 if 0 <= pos < len(vec):

              S_new=S+delta
              if S_new < 1e-12: continue

              if sc < best_sc_local:

              S=best_S

`optimize_at_n` code:
```
def optimize_at_n(n, parent, max_seconds, seed_offset=0):
    start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+max_seconds
    seqs=initial_sequences(n,parent)
    # score seqs but stop if time; keep top list
    scored=[]
    for s in seqs:
       if time > limit-30: break
       sc=compute_score(s); if finite: scored.append((sc,s))
    scored.sort()
    # merge top 10 unique by score maybe
    if not scored: a=make_p_n(n); sc=score
    best_a,best_sc=scored[0]
    # Time-flatten top up to 8 seeds with allocated time
    top=scored[:8]
    # first, if best_sc finite, maybe run best first? We'll run each seed for equal chunk.
    seg_time = max(20, (limit - start - 600) / max(1,len(top))) # leave 600 for lse/coord
    for idx,(sc_seed,s) in enumerate(top):
       if time > limit-600: break
       chunk = min(seg_time, limit-start? use time.monotonic)
       a_out,sc_out=time_flatten(s, max_seconds=chunk, seed=seed_offset+idx)
       if sc_out<best_sc: update
    # LSE budget: 450s
    if time < limit-120:
       lse_budget=min(450, limit-time-120)
       a_lse,sc_lse=optimize_lse(best_a, lse_budget, seed=seed_offset+100)
       update
    # Coordinate budget: 250s
    if time < limit-60:
       coord_budget=min(250, limit-time-60)
       a_coord,sc_coord=coordinate_descent(best_a, coord_budget, seed=seed_offset+200)
       update
    # Final time_flatten from best using remaining
    if time < limit-10:
       rem=limit-time-10
       a_final,sc_final=time_flatten(best_a, rem, seed=seed_offset+300)
       update
    return best_a,best_sc
```
This leaves in `optimize_at_n` maybe 600+450+250+final? Total could exceed max_seconds but loops conditional. More deterministic: Use fractions: `time_for_tl = 0.55*max_seconds`, `lse=0.25`, `coord=0.15`, final 5%. We'll implement with time checks relative to start.

coord_limit=start+0.95*max_seconds
```

Detailed code:

`make_p_exact`:
```
def make_p_exact(n, rev=False):
    a=np.empty(n, float)
    a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        a[i]=a[i-1]*(2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i)
    if rev: a=a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize(a)
```
Loop n=20000 fine.

`make_p_offset(n, offset=0.5, rev=False)`: `(np.arange(n)+offset)**-0.5`.

`initial_sequences`:
```
def initial_sequences(n, parent):
    seqs=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p=normalize(interpolate_to_n(parent,n))
       seqs.append(p)
       seqs.append(normalize(p[::-1].copy()))
       rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
       for sc in (0.01,0.03,0.06): seqs.append(normalize(p+sc*rng.standard_normal(n)))
    p=make_p_exact(n)
    seqs.append(p); seqs.append(normalize(p[::-1].copy())); seqs.append(normalize(0.5*p+0.5*p[::-1]))
    seqs.append(make_p_offset(n,0.5)); seqs.append(normalize(make_p_offset(n,0.5)[::-1]))
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float); xn_safe=1e-5+(x+0.5)/n
    for e in (-0.5,-0.35,-0.2,-0.1,0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0):
        a=np.power(xn_safe, e) if e!=0 else np.ones(n)
        seqs.append(normalize(a)); seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))
    for alpha in (0.5,1,2,5):
       a=np.exp(-alpha*xn)
       seqs.append(normalize(a)); seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))
    # modulations of p
    for eps in (0.05,0.15,-0.05,-0.15):
       mod=1.0+eps*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*xn)
       seqs.append(normalize(p*mod))
       mod2=1.0+eps*np.cos(4.0*np.pi*xn)
       seqs.append(normalize(p*mod2))
    # random low density
    rng2=np.random.default_rng(3)
    for rho in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
       mask=rng2.random(n)<rho
       if mask.any(): seqs.append(normalize(mask.astype(float)))
    return seqs
```
For e=0 `np.power` would ones but avoid 0^0? use branch. For negative e and xn_safe positive. Good.

Could have duplicates; fine.

large_a,sc_large=optimize_at_n(large_n, best_a, large_budget)
update
# final polish best with remaining if >100
```
If budget budget_s not exactly 2400? Use given.

   rng=...,start...

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2n * max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The optimizer combines:
  - strong analytic starting points (inverse-square-root / polynomial-square-root seeds),
  - time-domain convolution flattening via spectral Newton projection,
  - LSE-smoothed L-BFGS in log/softmax parameters,
  - exact coordinate descent with rank-one convolution updates,
  - coarse-to-fine length search and candidate mixing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def normalize(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return x
    return x / s


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:L]
    return c


def compute_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def interpolate_to_n(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == n:
        return x.copy()
    if x.size <= 1:
        return np.full(n, float(x[0]) if x.size else 1.0)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, x)


def make_p_exact(n, rev=False):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    if rev:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize(a)


def make_p_offset(n, offset=0.5, rev=False):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + max(offset, 1e-6)
    a = np.power(x, -0.5)
    if abs(a[0]) < 1e-300:
        a[0] = 1.0
    if rev:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize(a)


def initial_sequences(n, parent):
    seqs = []

    if parent is not None:
        p = normalize(interpolate_to_n(parent, n))
        if p.size == n and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
            seqs.append(p.copy())
            seqs.append(normalize(p[::-1].copy()))
            rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
            for sc in (0.008, 0.02, 0.05):
                seqs.append(normalize(p + sc * rng.standard_normal(n)))

    p = make_p_exact(n)
    seqs.append(p.copy())
    seqs.append(normalize(p[::-1].copy()))
    seqs.append(normalize(0.5 * p + 0.5 * p[::-1]))
    seqs.append(make_p_offset(n, 0.5, False))
    seqs.append(make_p_offset(n, 0.5, True))

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = (x + 0.5) / float(n)
    xn_safe = 1e-6 + xn

    for e in (-0.55, -0.45, -0.35, -0.25, -0.15, 0.0, 0.2, 0.45, 0.8):
        if e == 0.0:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a = np.power(xn_safe, e)
        seqs.append(normalize(a))
        seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        a = np.exp(-alpha * xn)
        seqs.append(normalize(a))
        seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))

    for eps in (0.04, 0.10, -0.04, -0.10):
        mod = 1.0 + eps * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * xn)
        seqs.append(normalize(p * mod))
        mod = 1.0 + eps * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * xn)
        seqs.append(normalize(p * mod))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    for rho in (0.03, 0.06, 0.12):
        mask = rng.random(n) < rho
        if mask.any():
            seqs.append(normalize(mask.astype(np.float64)))

    for center, sigma in ((0.12, 0.10), (0.30, 0.18), (0.50, 0.22), (0.70, 0.16)):
        a = np.exp(-((xn - center) * (xn - center)) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma))
        seqs.append(normalize(a))
        seqs.append(normalize(a[::-1].copy()))

    return seqs


def make_target(c, mode, prog, rng):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    L = c.size
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    c = c / s

    if mode == 0:
        q = 0.78 + 0.20 * min(1.0, max(0.0, prog))
        T = float(np.quantile(c, min(0.999, max(0.001, q))))
        c = np.minimum(c, T)
        c += (1.0 - c.sum()) / float(L)

    elif mode == 1:
        alpha = 0.60 + 0.30 * min(1.0, max(0.0, prog))
        c = np.power(np.maximum(c, 0.0), alpha)
        c += 1e-30
        c /= c.sum()

    elif mode == 2:
        lam = 0.05 + 0.55 * min(1.0, max(0.0, prog))
        c = (1.0 - lam) * c + lam / float(L)
        c += 1e-30
        c /= c.sum()

    elif mode == 3:
        q = 0.90
        T = float(np.quantile(c, q))
        c2 = np.minimum(c, T)
        deficit = 1.0 - float(c2.sum())
        low_count = max(1, int(0.45 * L))
        low_idx = np.argpartition(c2, low_count)[:low_count]
        c2[low_idx] += deficit / float(low_count)
        c = c2
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        c /= c.sum()

    else:
        c = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    ss = float(c.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return c / ss


def time_flatten(a0, max_seconds, seed=1):
    a = normalize(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, compute_score(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(max_seconds, 1.0)

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    modes = [0, 2, 1, 3]
    last_print = -100

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        c = fast_conv(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s)

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            if it - last_print > 40:
                print(f"    time-flatten new best: {sc:.8f} it={it}")
                last_print = it
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 160:
            a = normalize(best_a + 0.003 * (1.0 / n) * rng.standard_normal(n))
            no_improve = 40

        if time.monotonic() > limit - 1.0:
            break

        prog = min(1.0, it / 400.0)
        cn = c / c.sum() if c.sum() > 0 else c
        B = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        best_cand = None
        best_cand_sc = best_sc

        for mode in (modes[it % 4], modes[(it + 1) % 4]):
            ct = make_target(cn, mode, prog, rng)
            Ct = np.fft.rfft(ct, M)
            worst = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(B))))
            mask = np.abs(B) > worst
            Bnew = B.copy()
            if np.any(mask):
                Bnew[mask] = 0.5 * (B[mask] + Ct[mask] / B[mask])

            for damp in (1.0, 0.35):
                Bupd = B + damp * (Bnew - B)
                cand = np.fft.irfft(Bupd, M)[:n]
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                sp = float(cand.sum())
                if sp <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = cand / sp
                sc_c = compute_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_cand_sc:
                    best_cand_sc = sc_c
                    best_cand = cand.copy()

        if best_cand is not None:
            accept = (best_cand_sc < sc - 1e-14) or (best_cand_sc < best_sc) or (it < 25)
            if accept:
                a = normalize(best_cand)
                if best_cand_sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = best_cand_sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
            elif no_improve > 70:
                a = normalize(0.80 * a + 0.20 * best_cand)
                sc2 = compute_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
        it += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def lse_score_grad_a(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    c = fast_conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    sh = beta * (c - cmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -500.0, 500.0)
    ex = np.exp(sh)
    ex_sum = float(ex.sum())
    if ex_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ex_sum):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = ex / ex_sum
    L = cmax + np.log(ex_sum) / beta

    a_rev = a[::-1]
    M3 = (2 * n - 1) + (n - 1)
    M3f = 1 << (M3 - 1).bit_length()
    cp = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p, M3f) * np.fft.rfft(a_rev, M3f), M3f)[:M3]
    grad_L = 2.0 * cp[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    s2 = s * s
    s3 = s2 * s
    grad = 2.0 * n * (grad_L / s2 - (2.0 * L / s3) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    score = float(2.0 * n * L / s2)
    return score, grad


def softmax_b(b):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.clip(b, -50.0, 50.0)
    b = b - np.mean(b)
    b = b - np.max(b)
    a = np.exp(b)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(b, dtype=np.float64) / b.size
    return a / s


def lse_b_objective(b, beta):
    a = softmax_b(b)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_a(a, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e30, np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
    h = ga - float(np.dot(a, ga))
    g = a * h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
    return sc, g


def optimize_lse(a0, max_seconds, seed=1):
    a = normalize(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(max_seconds, 1.0)

    b = np.where(a > 1e-300, np.log(a + 1e-300), -30.0)
    b = b - np.mean(b)
    b = np.clip(b, -50.0, 50.0)

    betas = [1e3, 1e4, 5e4, 2e5, 1e6]
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 45:
            break
        rem = limit - time.monotonic() - 40
        if rem < 10:
            break
        maxiter = int(min(3500, max(120, 80 + rem * 2.5)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_b_objective,
                b,
                args=(beta,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-18,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxiter * 20,
                    "maxls": 40,
                },
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                ax = softmax_b(res.x)
                scx = compute_score(ax)
                if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_sc:
                    best_sc = scx
                    best_a = ax.copy()
                    b = np.where(ax > 1e-300, np.log(ax + 1e-300), -30.0)
                    b = b - np.mean(b)
                    b = np.clip(b, -50.0, 50.0)
                    print(f"    LSE beta={beta:.1e} new best: {best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a, best_sc


def coordinate_descent(a0, max_seconds, seed=0):
    a = normalize(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.maximum(fast_conv(a), 0.0)
    S = 1.0
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(max_seconds, 1.0)

    top_peaks = 24
    top_coords = 90
    no_pass = 0

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            break
        scale = 1.0 / s
        if abs(scale - 1.0) > 1e-8:
            a = a * scale
            c = c * (scale * scale)
            S = 1.0

        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        sc_cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c) / (S * S))
        if sc_cur < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_cur
            best_a = normalize(a.copy())

        if top_peaks >= L:
            peak_idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            peak_idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_peaks)[-top_peaks:]

        weights = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in peak_idx:
            k = int(k)
            st = max(0, k - n + 1)
            en = min(k, n - 1)
            if en < st:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(st, en + 1)
            weights[idx] += a[idx] * a[k - idx]

        if n > top_coords + 40:
            cand_idx = np.argpartition(weights, -top_coords)[-top_coords:]
            extra = rng.choice(n, size=40, replace=False)
            cand_idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand_idx, extra)))
        else:
            cand_idx = np.argsort(weights)[-n:]

        improved = False
        for j in cand_idx:
            j = int(j)
            v = float(a[j])
            if v < 1e-12:
                base = S / float(n)
                w_vals = base * (0.001, 0.01, 0.1, 1.0)
            else:
                w_vals = v * (0.50, 0.75, 0.90, 1.05, 1.25, 1.70)

            best_w = None
            best_vec = None
            best_S = S
            best_local = sc_cur

            for w in w_vals:
                w = float(w)
                if w < 0.0:
                    continue
                delta = w - v
                if delta == 0.0:
                    continue
                idx0 = max(0, j - n + 1)
                idx1 = min(j + n - 1, L - 1)
                if idx1 < idx0:
                    continue
                m = idx0 - j + np.arange(idx1 - idx0 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                vec = 2.0 * delta * a[m]
                k2 = 2 * j
                if 0 <= k2 < L:
                    pos = k2 - idx0
                    if 0 <= pos < vec.size:
                        vec[pos] = delta * (v + w)
                S_new = S + delta
                if S_new <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                c_new = c + vec
                sc_new = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c_new) / (S_new * S_new))
                if sc_new < best_local:
                    best_local = sc_new
                    best_w = w
                    best_vec = vec
                    best_S = S_new

            if best_vec is not None and best_local < sc_cur - 1e-12:
                a[j] = best_w
                c = c + best_vec
                S = best_S
                sc_cur = best_local
                improved = True
                if sc_cur < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_cur
                    best_a = normalize(a.copy())

        if not improved:
            no_pass += 1
            if no_pass >= 5:
                a = normalize(a + 0.002 * (S / float(n)) * rng.standard_normal(n))
                c = np.maximum(fast_conv(a), 0.0)
                S = 1.0
                sc_cur = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
                no_pass = 0
        else:
            no_pass = 0

    return best_a, float(compute_score(best_a))


def mix_candidates(base, candidates, max_seconds=30, seed=0):
    a = normalize(np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(max_seconds, 1.0)

    for cand in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 8:
            break
        c = normalize(interpolate_to_n(cand, n))
        for t in (0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9):
            ax = normalize(t * best_a + (1.0 - t) * c)
            sc = compute_score(ax)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = ax.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def optimize_at_n(n, parent, max_seconds, seed_offset=0, extra=None):
    n = int(max(64, min(25000, n)))
    start = time.monotonic()
    limit = start + max(max_seconds, 1.0)

    seqs = initial_sequences(n, parent)
    if extra:
        for e in extra:
            if e is not None:
                seqs.append(normalize(interpolate_to_n(e, n)))

    scored = []
    if max_seconds < 35:
        for s in seqs[:16]:
            sc = compute_score(s)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, s))
        if not scored:
            a = make_p_exact(n)
            return a, compute_score(a)
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        return scored[0][1], scored[0][0]

    for s in seqs:
        if time.monotonic() > limit - 30:
            break
        sc = compute_score(s)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, s))

    if not scored:
        a = make_p_exact(n)
        sc = compute_score(a)
        return a, sc

    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    best_a, best_sc = scored[0]
    top = scored[:8]

    tl_limit = start + 0.55 * (limit - start)
    lse_limit = start + 0.80 * (limit - start)
    coord_limit = start + 0.955 * (limit - start)

    print(f"  opt n={n}: initial best {best_sc:.8f}, seeds={len(scored)}")

    for idx, (sc_seed, seq) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() > tl_limit - 5:
            break
        remaining_seeds = len(top) - idx
        chunk = (tl_limit - time.monotonic()) / float(max(1, remaining_seeds))
        chunk = float(min(260.0, max(25.0, chunk)))
        a_out, sc_out = time_flatten(seq, chunk, seed=seed_offset + 1 + idx)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_out
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            print(f"    n={n} time-flatten top{idx}: {best_sc:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < lse_limit - 30:
        a_out, sc_out = optimize_lse(best_a, lse_limit - time.monotonic(), seed=seed_offset + 100)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_out
            best_a = a_out.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < coord_limit - 20:
        a_out, sc_out = coordinate_descent(best_a, coord_limit - time.monotonic(), seed=seed_offset + 200)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_out
            best_a = a_out.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < limit - 10:
        a_out, sc_out = mix_candidates(best_a, [s for _, s in top[:6]],
                                       max_seconds=min(25.0, limit - time.monotonic()))
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_out
            best_a = a_out.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 35.0)

    parent = None
    try:
        hp = height_sequence_1
        if hp is not None:
            p = np.asarray(hp, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 32 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = normalize(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = None

    def record(score_val, arr, label):
        nonlocal best_score, best_a
        if arr is None:
            return
        arr = np.clip(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size <= 0 or float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-9:
            return
        score_val = float(score_val)
        if np.isfinite(score_val) and score_val < best_score:
            best_score = score_val
            best_a = normalize(arr)
            print(f"  NEW BEST [{label}]: {best_score:.8f} len={best_a.size} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    if parent is not None:
        record(compute_score(parent), parent, "parent")

    main_n = 10000
    if parent is not None:
        lp = parent.size
        if 4096 <= lp <= 16000:
            main_n = max(8192, min(16384, int(round(lp / 1024.0) * 1024)))

    extra = []
    verbose = True

    if verbose:
        print(f"  proposed main_n={main_n}, deadline in {deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}s")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 360:
        coarse_n = 512
        coarse_budget = min(150.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 320.0)
        if verbose:
            print(f"  coarse optimize n={coarse_n} budget={coarse_budget:.0f}s")
        a_c, sc_c = optimize_at_n(coarse_n, parent, coarse_budget, seed_offset=10000)
        record(sc_c, a_c, "coarse")
        extra.append(a_c)

    main_budget = min(1750.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 560.0)
    if main_budget > 45:
        if verbose:
            print(f"  main optimize n={main_n} budget={main_budget:.0f}s")
        a_m, sc_m = optimize_at_n(main_n, parent, main_budget, seed_offset=seed, extra=extra)
        record(sc_m, a_m, "main")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 260:
        large_n = min(20000, max(main_n + 4096, 2 * main_n))
        large_budget = min(700.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 120.0)
        if large_budget > 60:
            if verbose:
                print(f"  large optimize n={large_n} budget={large_budget:.0f}s")
            a_l, sc_l = optimize_at_n(large_n, best_a, large_budget, seed_offset=20000)
            record(sc_l, a_l, "large")

    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
       a_p, sc_p = time_flatten(best_a, max_seconds=max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10),
                                      seed=seed + 30000)
        record(sc_p, a_p, "final-flatten")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = normalize(np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64))
        best_score = compute_score(best_a)

    if best_a.size > 25000:
        best_a = normalize(interpolate_to_n(best_a, 20000))
        best_score = compute_score(best_a)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    print("=" * 64)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.8f} verified={verified:.8f}")
    print(f"length={len(out)} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.2f}s budget={budget_s}s")
    print("=" * 64)
    return out
```
